Date: 2018-07-09 21:00:05
Tweet: A head in the clouds
And a heavy heart of words
Is all I have become.
Thank yous and swords,
Punishments, rewards;
A silence I cannot overcome.
Swallowed in mute
That is so absolute
It shames all eloquence and rhyme.
A past I forsake
And a future to make
That is as dead as time.

Date: 2018-07-09 20:44:25
Tweet: Black hour glasses and braided rivers,
Endings that are unseen.
Realities that writhe and sliver
Upon will, desire and means.

I am what I want and not. 
You are what you cannot. 
We are everything and naught; 
Unseen, in between, obscene.
